# Python API parity inventory

This table is generated from a reconciled AST and runtime inventory of
libtmux revision [`c4a980b`](https://github.com/tmux-python/libtmux/tree/c4a980b).
It includes every public declaration, class member, assignment, enum value,
explicit package re-export, inspected signature, default, and deprecation
found across all package modules. Runtime discovery independently inspects
each imported module namespace; structured export rules then reconcile those
names with conditional-aware AST declarations. The disposition follows the architecture
package map; an entry is not an implementation claim.

The contract test column names the test each row requires, in the naming scheme
those tests will use. It is planned parity, in the sense the
[test map](test-map.md) gives that phrase, and not a citation: no row here is
evidence that the test it names has been written. Java symbols named in the
table are likewise the intended shape rather than a report of what exists;
`docs/guide/` describes what does.

Inventory result: 33 modules, 889 reconciled symbols; 884 seen by both passes, 5 AST-only, and 0 runtime-generated.
